Dropdrop — Third-party notices
========================================================================

Reviewed: 2026-11-17
External package versions: 282
Lockfile SHA-256: ca4e6de97182d1cb533dbcdc1e6a7b26b3df82b27c248d7269414c61eb8ab946

Scope: every external package entry in package-lock.json, including optional
platform and development dependencies. Installed package metadata and supplied
license documents were inspected. Missing optional platform packages were
downloaded and integrity-checked without installing or executing their code.

Distribution notes:
- Include LICENSE, NOTICE and this file in npm tarballs and other distributions.
- Vue runtime code is included in the web bundle even though Vue is declared
  as a development dependency. Preserve its MIT copyright and license in the
  JavaScript delivered to browsers as well as in this file.
- better-sqlite3 is MIT; its bundled SQLite source has a public-domain notice,
  preserved below. This does not relicense SQLite under Apache-2.0.
- Lightning CSS and its platform binaries are MPL-2.0 build dependencies.
  Using them to build CSS does not by itself apply MPL to application code.
  Redistributing these tools or modified MPL files requires their own notices
  and covered source availability, independently of Dropdrop's license.
- Vite and other tools ship additional bundled-component notices. Those
  supplied documents are preserved as part of this inventory.

This is a package-level license and notice review. It does not certify the
provenance of every source line or reverse-engineer all prebuilt native/WASM
toolchain binaries. Review new versions and release contents before shipping.

These are third-party declarations, not additional licenses for the
original Dropdrop code. Each component keeps its own license.
Alternative license expressions are reproduced as declared by upstream;
this does not relicense a dependency. For a permissive OR option, an
Apache-2.0 or MIT option may be used; AND conditions remain cumulative.
Repeated license documents are reproduced once in the appendix and
referenced by a content-derived identifier. Package author metadata
is retained for attribution; it is not an assertion of copyright ownership.

------------------------------------------------------------------------
# `@humanfs/types`

by [Nicholas C. Zakas](https://humanwhocodes.com)

If you find this useful, please consider supporting my work with a [donation](https://humanwhocodes.com/donate) or [nominate me](https://stars.github.com/nominate/) for a GitHub Star.

## Description

Additional type definitions for humanfs. These are used primarily to share type definitions across packages because there is no easy way to export imported type definitions using JavaScript and JSDoc.
